- Recorded transcript prompt text can trigger encoded-exfiltration, instruction-policy and persistence patterns. These are inert historical evidence, preserved for review; no payload runs on plugin import.
- Private imports in demo/runtime.py are opt-in harness integration, not installed runtime hooks.

- The automatic isolation label does not establish plugin-host compatibility. Supported/tested operation remains in_process, including the CLI command.
